.

5.
Conclusions

In this paper, we study inference in the context of a factor model for a high-dimensional matrix-valued time series with the possible presence of
common stochastic trends and common stationary factors. The inferential problem is not a simple extension of existing techniques: the presence of
the common stationary factors makes it impossible to refine the rates of the estimators of the non-stationary common factor structure using e.g. the iterated projection-based estimator of
Yu
et al. (2022)
. Hence, we propose an entirely novel procedure, based on a preliminary step where the common stochastic trends are eliminated by projecting them away onto a large dimensional space constructed as the orthogonal complement to the loadings space of the
I(1)
common factor structure. As mentioned in the introduction, this step goes in the opposite direction to the projection-based estimator, and we view it as an “anti-projection” (or an “anti-Johnson-Lindenstrauss”) argument. After getting rid of the common
I(1)
components, we estimate the common stationary component in a standard way, and after removing it from the data, we are able to use the full force of the iterative projection-based estimator. Our results, in terms of the rates of convergence of the estimated loadings and common factors, show that we are able to refine the rates of convergence of the estimators of the
I(1)
factor structure. As a by-product, we also propose a technique to estimate the number of common factors in both the
I(1)
and the stationary structures; further, building on the results on the spectrum of the second moment matrices studied in this paper, it would also be possible to propose several tests, along e.g. the lines of
Barigozzi and
Trapani (2022)
, for the null hypothesis that the data are
I(1)
, which would serve as a preliminary step to ascertain whether our estimation technique needs to be applied (i.e., whether
\mathbf{X}_{t}
is indeed
I(1)
), or not.

Several interesting questions and possible extensions are still outstanding. In addition to deriving the limiting distributions of the estimated
loadings, common factors and common components (which, in principle, can be done as an extension of our results), and to extending our results to the presence of deterministic components (such as drifts or linear trends in the common
I(1)
structure), here we revisit the notion of
cointegration and how this can be cast into our model (
1
). In particular, we note that our current model and theory can only partly embed
a (Matrix) Error Correction Model (
Johansen, 1991
). Indeed, consider again the two-step hierarchical representation discussed in
the introduction, and, unless stated otherwise, assume for simplicity that
h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=1
. Recalling that
\mathbf{X}_{\cdot j,t}
,
1\leq j\leq p_{2}
denotes the
j
-th column of
\mathbf{X}_{t}
, consider the MA
(\infty)
representation
\Delta\mathbf{X}_{\cdot j,t}=\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j}\left(L\right)\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\cdot j,t}
, where
\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j}\left(L\right)=\sum_{h=0}^{\infty}\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j,h}L^{h}
is a
p_{1}\times p_{1}
-valued MA
(\infty)
polynomial. Then, by standard arguments,
2
2

2

See e.g.
Watson (1994)
we can represent
\mathbf{X}_{\cdot j,t}
as

(33)

\mathbf{X}_{\cdot j,t}=\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j}(1)\sum_{s=1}^{t}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\cdot j,s}+\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j}^{\ast}\left(L\right)\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\cdot j,t},

where
\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j}(1)=\sum_{h=0}^{\infty}\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j,h}
is a
p_{1}\times p_{1}
matrix with rank
h_{R_{1}}
, and
\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j}^{\ast}\left(L\right)=\sum_{h=0}^{\infty}\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j,h}^{\ast}L^{h}
with
\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j,h}^{\ast}=-\sum_{k=h+1}^{\infty}\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j,k}
. Hence,
\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j}(1)
can be rewritten as the product between a
p_{1}\times h_{R_{1}}
matrix (say
\mathbf{R}_{1}
) and an
h_{R_{1}}\times p_{1}
matrix (say
\mathbf{\Pi}_{j}
), whence (
33
) becomes

\mathbf{X}_{\cdot j,t}=\underset{p_{1}\times k_{R}}{\mathbf{R}_{1}}\underset{k_{R}\times p_{1}}{\mathbf{\Pi}_{j}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\cdot j,s}+\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j}^{\ast}\left(L\right)\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\cdot j,t}.

Define, for short, the scalar common trend
\mathbf{G}_{j,t}=\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{j}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\cdot j,s}
, and consider the
p_{2}\times 1
vector of common trends
\mathbf{G}_{t}=\left(\mathbf{G}_{1,t},...,\mathbf{G}_{p_{2},t}\right)^{\prime}
. The vector
\mathbf{G}_{t}
itself could be cointegrated; considering the MA
(\infty)
representation
\Delta\mathbf{G}_{t}=\mathbf{\Gamma}_{G}\left(L\right)\mathbf{u}_{t}
, it follows that

(34)

\mathbf{G}_{t}=\mathbf{\Gamma}_{G}(1)\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{u}_{s}+\mathbf{\Gamma}_{G}^{\ast}\left(L\right)\mathbf{u}_{t}.

Again,
\mathbf{\Gamma}_{G}(1)
is
p_{2}\times p_{2}
and has rank
h_{C_{1}}=1
, so that we can write

\mathbf{G}_{t}=\mathbf{\Gamma}_{G}(1)\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{u}_{s}+\mathbf{\Gamma}_{G}^{\ast}\left(L\right)\mathbf{u}_{t}=\underset{p_{2}\times h_{C_{1}}}{\mathbf{C}_{1}}\underset{h_{C_{1}}\times p_{2}}{\mathbf{\beta}_{G}^{\prime}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{u}_{s}+\mathbf{\Gamma}_{G}^{\ast}\left(L\right)\mathbf{u}_{t}=\underset{p_{2}\times h_{C_{1}}}{\mathbf{C}_{1}}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{\Gamma}_{G}^{\ast}\left(L\right)\mathbf{u}_{t},

where
\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}=\mathbf{\beta}_{G}^{\prime}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{u}_{s}
is the common stochastic trend. Then, by substituting, we
have

\mathbf{X}_{t}=\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{G}_{t}^{\prime}+\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}_{t},

where
\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}_{t}
is a stationary matrix-valued whose
j
-th column is given by
\mathbf{\Gamma}_{j}^{\ast}\left(L\right)\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\cdot j,t}
in (
33
); using (
34
), we receive

(35)

\mathbf{X}_{t}=\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}+\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\prime}\left[\mathbf{\Gamma}_{G}^{\ast}(1)\right]^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t},

where
\mathbf{E}_{t}
is the overall error term. In this model, we have the same structure for the common stochastic trend(s)
\mathbf{F}_{1,t}
as in
model (
1
). However, the common stationary component
\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\prime}\left[\mathbf{\Gamma}_{G}^{\ast}\left(L\right)\right]^{\prime}
is different to the one in (
1
), in that
\mathbf{\Gamma}_{G}^{\ast}(1)
may have full rank, thus entailing that the stationary common component
\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\prime}\left[\mathbf{\Gamma}_{G}^{\ast}(1)\right]^{\prime}
does not have a “two-way” but a “one-way” structure. Our assumptions hereafter are also different to the ones implicitly present in (
35
), seeing as we assume that
\mathbf{F}_{1,t}
and
\mathbf{F}_{0,t}
are independent, whereas, in (
35
),
\mathbf{F}_{1,t}
and
\mathbf{u}_{t}
clearly are not. Hence, the extension of our methodology to a cointegrated system - whilst building on the methodology developed herein - is a not entirely trivial task, which is currently under investigation by the authors.

As a second extension, augmenting (
1
)-(
2
) to include linear trends could be also of interest. In such a case, (
1
) would become

(36)

\mathbf{X}_{t}=\mathbf{A}t\mathbf{B}^{\prime}+\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}+\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}.

An “anti-projection” based estimation strategy, in this case, could be based on estimating
\mathbf{C}_{1}
and
\mathbf{C}_{0}
from the first-differenced version of (
36
)

\displaystyle\Delta\mathbf{X}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{AB}^{\prime}+\mathbf{R}_{1}\Delta\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}+\mathbf{R}_{0}\Delta\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}+\Delta\mathbf{E}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{AB}^{\prime}+\mathbf{R}\Delta\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}+\Delta\mathbf{E}_{t},

using e.g. the projection-based estimator of
Yu
et al. (2022)
(denoting this as, say,
\hat{\mathbf{R}}\
and
\hat{\mathbf{C}}
), and subsequently
estimating
\mathbf{A}
from the anti-projected version of
\mathbf{X}_{t}
, viz.
\mathbf{X}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{\perp}
. This extension, as well
as the case where one of the common
I(1)
factor has a drift component, is also under investigation by the authors.

Appendix A
Technical lemmas

Henceforth, whenever possible in this and in the next section, for
simplicity and without loss of generality we will carry out our proofs under
the constraints
h_{R_{1}}=1
,
h_{C_{1}}=1
,
h_{R_{0}}=1
, and
h_{C_{0}}=1
.

Lemma 5
.

Consider a multi-index random variable
U_{i_{1},\dots,i_{h}}
,
with
1\leq i_{1}\leq S_{1}
,
1\leq i_{2}\leq S_{2}
, etc…Assume that

(37)

\sum_{S_{1}}\cdot\cdot\sum_{S_{h}}\frac{1}{S_{1}\cdot\dots\cdot S_{h}}P\left(\max_{1\leq i_{1}\leq S_{1},\dots,1\leq i_{h}\leq S_{h}}\left|U_{i_{1},\dots,i_{h}}\right|>\epsilon L_{S_{1},\dots,S_{h}}\right)<\infty,

for some
\epsilon>0
and a sequence
L_{S_{1},\dots,S_{h}}
defined as

L_{S_{1},\dots,S_{h}}=S_{1}^{d_{1}}\cdot\dots\cdot S_{h}^{d_{h}}l_{1}\left(S_{1}\right)\cdot\dots l_{h}\left(S_{h}\right),

where
d_{1}
,
d_{2}
, etc. are non-negative numbers and
l_{1}\left(\cdot\right)
,
l_{2}\left(\cdot\right)
, etc. are slowly varying functions
in the sense of Karamata. Then it holds that

(38)

\limsup_{\left(S_{1},\dots,S_{h}\right)\rightarrow\infty}\frac{\left|U_{S_{1},\dots,S_{h}}\right|}{L_{S_{1},\dots,S_{h}}}=0\text{ {a.s.}}

Proof.

The lemma is shown in
Massacci and
Trapani (2022)
- see in particular Lemma A11
therein.
∎

Lemma 6
.

We assume that Assumption
1
is satisfied. Then it
holds that

(39)

E\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\leq c_{0}t,

(40)

\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\left(T^{2}\right)

Proof.

Equation (
39
) follows immediately from Proposition 4 in
Berkes
et al. (2011)
. As far as (
40
) is concerned, it follows
immediately from the FCLT for Bernoulli shifts (e.g., Theorem A.1 in
Aue
et al., 2009
) and the Continuous Mapping Theorem.
∎

Lemma 7
.

We assume that Assumption
1
is satisfied. Then it
holds that

(41)

\displaystyle\liminf_{T\rightarrow\infty}\frac{\log\log T}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=D_{1}\text{ \ \ a.s.},

(42)

\displaystyle\liminf_{T\rightarrow\infty}\frac{\log\log T}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle=D_{2}\text{ \ \ a.s.},

where
D_{1}
and
D_{2}
are two positive definite matrices of dimensions
h_{R_{1}}\times h_{R_{1}}
and
h_{C_{1}}\times h_{C_{1}}\
respectively.

Proof.

We prove (
41
) - the proof of (
42
) is the same.
Assumption
1
entails that, for each
T
, it is possible to define a
matrix valued,
h_{R_{1}}\times h_{C_{1}}
-dimensional Wiener process
\left\{\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(k\right),1\leq k\leq T\right\}
, with covariance
matrix
\Sigma_{F}^{\left(a\right)}
such that

(43)

\max_{1\leq k\leq T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,k}-\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(k\right)\right\|=O_{a.s.}\left(T^{1/2-\zeta}\right),

for some
\zeta>0
(a proof can be found e.g. in
Aue et al., 2014
).
Considering

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}^{\prime}\left(t\right)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}-\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\right)\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}^{\prime}\left(t\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}-\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\right)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}-\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\right)\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}-\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\right)^{\prime},

equation (
43
) entails

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}-\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\right)\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}^{\prime}\left(t\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}-\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\right\|\left\|\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\right\|\leq T\max_{1\leq k\leq T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,k}-\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\right\|\max_{1\leq k\leq T}\left\|\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=T\cdot O_{a.s.}\left(T^{1/2-\zeta}\right)O_{a.s.}\left(T^{1/2}\left(\log\log T\right)^{1/2}\right)=O_{a.s.}\left(T^{2-\zeta}\left(\log\log T\right)^{1/2}\right),

and by the same token

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}-\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\right)\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}-\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\right)^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq T\left(\max_{1\leq k\leq T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,k}-\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\right\|\right)^{2}=O_{a.s.}\left(T^{2-2\zeta}\right),

whence

\frac{\log\log T}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}=\frac{\log\log T}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}^{\prime}\left(t\right)+o_{a.s.}(1).

Note now that, letting
\mathbf{W}_{RC,T,j}\left(t\right)
be the
j
-th
column of
\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)

\frac{\log\log T}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}^{\prime}\left(t\right)=\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\frac{\log\log T}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{W}_{RC,T,j}\left(t\right)\mathbf{W}_{RC,T,j}^{\prime}\left(t\right).

We now show that the limit of the expression above is positive definite. To
this end, let
\mathbf{b}
be a
h_{C_{1}}\times 1
nontrivial vector and
consider

\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\frac{\log\log T}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{b}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{RC,T,j}\left(t\right)\mathbf{W}_{RC,T,j}^{\prime}\left(t\right)\mathbf{b};

given that the distribution of
\mathbf{W}_{RC,T}\left(t\right)
,does not
depend on
T
, we have that
\left\{\mathbf{b}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{RC,T,j}\left(t\right)\right.,

\left.1\leq t\leq T\right\}

\overset{\mathcal{D}}{=}

\left\{\widetilde{W}_{j}\left(t\right),1\leq t\leq T\right\}
, where
\left\{\widetilde{W}_{j}\left(t\right),1\leq t\leq T\right\}
is a scalar Wiener process with variance such that

\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\mathbf{b}^{\prime}E\left(\mathbf{W}_{RC,T,j}\left(1\right)\mathbf{W}_{RC,T,j}^{\prime}(1)\right)\mathbf{b=b}^{\prime}\Sigma_{F}^{\left(a\right)}\mathbf{b.}

Using equation (4.6) in
Donsker and
Varadhan (1977)
, it therefore follows that

\liminf_{T\rightarrow\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\frac{\log\log T}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{b}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{RC,T,j}\left(t\right)\mathbf{W}_{RC,T,j}^{\prime}\left(t\right)\mathbf{b}=\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{b}^{\prime}\Sigma_{F}^{\left(a\right)}\mathbf{b},

a.s.; seeing as
\Sigma_{F}^{\left(a\right)}
is positive definite by
Assumption
1
(ii)
(a),
\mathbf{b}^{\prime}\Sigma_{F}^{\left(a\right)}\mathbf{b}>0
for all nontrivial
\mathbf{b}
. The
desired result now follows.
∎

Lemma 8
.

We assume that Assumption
2
is satisfied. Then it
holds that

(44)

\displaystyle\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left\|E\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,s}^{\prime}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq c_{0}T,\text{ and }\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left\|E\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{0,s}\right)\right\|_{F}\leq c_{1}T,

(45)

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{a.s.}\left(T\right),

for some
0<c_{0},c_{1}<\infty
.

Proof.

Equation (
44
) is a direct consequence of Assumption
2
- see in particular the proof of Lemma A.3 in
Massacci and
Trapani (2022)
. As far as (
45
) is concerned, we begin by showing that
\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}
is an
\mathcal{L}_{2}
-decomposable Bernoulli shift, focusing on the case
h_{R_{0}}=h_{C_{0}}=1
for simplicity and with no loss of generality. Consider the construction

\mathbf{F}_{0,t,t}^{\ast}=g_{\mathbf{F}_{0}}\left(\eta_{t}^{\mathbf{F}_{0}},\dots,\eta_{1}^{\mathbf{F}_{0}},\widetilde{\eta}_{0}^{\mathbf{F}_{0}},\widetilde{\eta}_{-1}^{\mathbf{F}_{0}},\dots\right).

It holds that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{2}-\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t,t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right|_{2}\leq\left|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}+\mathbf{F}_{0,t,t}^{\ast}\right|_{4}\left|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}-\mathbf{F}_{0,t,t}^{\ast}\right|_{4}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right|_{4}\left|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}-\mathbf{F}_{0,t,t}^{\ast}\right|_{4}\leq c_{0}^{-a},

which entails that
\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}
is a
\mathcal{L}_{2}
-decomposable Bernoulli shift. Hence, by Proposition 4 in
Berkes
et al. (2011)
, it follows that

E\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}-E\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)\right|^{2}\leq c_{0}T,

and therefore Theorem 3 in
Móricz (1976)
yields

E\max_{1\leq k\leq T}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}-E\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)\right|^{2}\leq c_{0}T\left(\log 2T\right)^{4}.

Equation (
45
) readily follows from the SLLN.
∎

Lemma 9
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
,
2
and
5
are satisfied. Then it holds that

\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(T\right).

Proof.

Clearly

\displaystyle E\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}=\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}E\left(F_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,s}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,s}\right)\leq\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left|E\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,s}\right)\right|\left|E\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,s}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left|\left(E\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(E\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,s}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\right|\left|E\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,s}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq c_{0}T\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left|E\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,s}\right)\right|\leq c_{1}T^{2},

having used Assumptions
1
and
2
, (
39
) in the
penultimate passage, and (
44
) in the last one. The desired
result now follows immediately from Markov inequality.
∎

Lemma 10
.

We assume that Assumption
3
are satisfied. Then it
holds that

(46)

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(p_{2}T\right)+O_{P}\left(p_{1}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}\right),

(47)

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(p_{1}T\right)+O_{P}\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T^{1/2}\right).

Proof.

We can write

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}=E\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right),

whence by Weyl’s inequality

\lambda_{\max}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\leq\lambda_{\max}\left(E\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)+\lambda_{\max}\left(\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\right)=I+II.

Using again Weyl’s inequality, it holds that

\lambda_{\max}\left(E\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\lambda_{\max}\left(E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right),

and by Assumption
3
(ii)
(b)

\lambda_{\max}\left(E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\leq\max_{1\leq i\leq p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{h=1}^{p_{1}}\left|E\left(e_{ij,t}e_{hj,t}\right)\right|\leq c_{0}p_{2},

whence finally it follows that
\lambda_{\max}\left(E\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)=O\left(p_{2}T\right)
. Also, by symmetry

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}\left(\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right\|_{F}=\left(\sum_{i,j=1}^{p_{1}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{p_{2}}\left(e_{ik,t}e_{jk,t}-E\left(e_{ik,t}e_{jk,t}\right)\right)\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

It holds that

\displaystyle E\sum_{i,j=1}^{p_{1}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{p_{2}}\left(e_{ik,t}e_{jk,t}-E\left(e_{ik,t}e_{jk,t}\right)\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{h,k=1}^{p_{2}}\cov\left(e_{ik,t}e_{jk,t},e_{ih,s}e_{jh,s}\right)\leq c_{0}p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T,

by Assumption
3
(iii)
(a). Hence by Markov inequality it
finally follows that

\lambda_{\max}\left(\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\right)=O_{P}\left(p_{1}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}\right),

whence (
46
) follows. Equation (
47
) follows from the same
logic.
∎

Lemma 11
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
,
3
and
5
are satisfied. Then it holds that

\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T\right).

Proof.

We let
h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=1
for simplicity. We have

\displaystyle E\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}e_{ij,t}\right)^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}E\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,s}\right)E\left(e_{ij,s}e_{ij,t}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}E\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{2}\right)\left|E\left(e_{ij,s}e_{ij,t}\right)\right|\leq c_{0}T\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left|E\left(e_{ij,s}e_{ij,t}\right)\right|\leq c_{1}p_{1}p_{2}T,

by Assumptions
5
(used in the second line) and
3
(ii)
(a), used in the third line.
∎

Lemma 12
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
are
satisfied. Then there exists a positive, finite constant
c_{0}
and a
triplet of random variables
\left(p_{0,0},p_{2,0},T_{0}\right)
such
that, for all
p_{1}\geq p_{0,0}
,
p_{2}\geq p_{2,0}
and
T\geq T_{0}
, it
holds that

\left(\log\log T\right)\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{M}_{R_{1}}\right)\geq c_{0}\text{, \ \ for all }j\leq h_{R_{1}},

and, for all
\epsilon>0

\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{M}_{R_{1}}\right)=o_{a.s.}\left(\frac{\left(\log T\right)^{3/2+\epsilon}}{T}\right),\text{ \ \ for all }j>h_{R_{1}}.

Proof.

It holds that

(48)

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{R_{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}np_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=I+II+III+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI+VI^{\prime}.

We begin by finding bounds for
II
,
III
,
IV
,
V
, and
VI
, using
h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=h_{R_{0}}=h_{C_{1}}=1
for simplicity whenever possible. We have

\left\|II\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{2}\right)=O_{a.s.}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right),

having used Assumption
4
(i)
(b) and (
45
) in
Lemma
8
. Turning to
III
, combining Lemmas
5
and
10
, it is easy to see that

\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}T}\right)+o_{a.s.}\left(\frac{\left(\log p_{1}\log p_{2}\log T\right)^{1+\epsilon}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right).

We now study

\left\|IV\right\|_{F}=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\left(\sum_{i,h=1}^{p_{1}}\left(r_{i}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}c_{j}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}e_{hj,t}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2};

it holds that

\displaystyle E\sum_{i,h=1}^{p_{1}}r_{i}^{2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}c_{j}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}e_{hj,t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,h=1}^{p_{1}}r_{i}^{2}E\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{j,k=1}^{p_{2}}c_{j}c_{k}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,s}e_{hj,t}e_{hk,s}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\max_{1\leq i\leq p_{1}}r_{i}^{2}\right)\left(\max_{1\leq i\leq p_{2}}c_{i}^{2}\right)\sum_{i,h=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{j,k=1}^{p_{2}}\left|\left(E\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(E\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,s}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\right|\left|E\left(e_{hj,t}e_{hk,s}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq c_{0}p_{1}T\sum_{h=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{j,k=1}^{p_{2}}\left|E\left(e_{hj,t}e_{hk,s}\right)\right|\leq c_{1}p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T^{2},

having used Assumption
5
in the third line, Assumption
4
(ii)
(d) in the last passage. Hence, using Lemma
5

\left\|IV\right\|_{F}=o_{a.s.}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}\left(\log p_{1}\log^{2}p_{2}\log^{2}T\right)^{1/2+\epsilon}\right),

for all
\epsilon>0
. The same bound holds, by symmetry, for
\left\|IV^{\prime}\right\|_{F}
. We now study

\left\|V\right\|_{F}\leq\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right)\leq c_{0}\frac{1}{T^{2}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right).

We have

\displaystyle E\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left(E\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(E\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,s}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left\|E\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,s}\right)\right\|_{F}\leq c_{0}T\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left\|E\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,s}\right)\right\|_{F}\leq c_{1}T^{2},

having used Assumption
5
and (
39
) in Lemma
6
and (
45
) in Lemma
8
in the last passage. Hence, by Lemma
5

\left\|V\right\|_{F}=o_{a.s.}\left(\frac{1}{T}\left(\log T\right)^{3/2+\epsilon}\right),

and the same bound holds, by symmetry, for
\left\|V^{\prime}\right\|_{F}
. Finally, we study

\displaystyle\left\|VI\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}.

It holds that

(49)

\displaystyle E\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=E\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}c_{1,j}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}e_{ij,t}\right)^{2}\right)=E\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j,k=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}c_{1,j}c_{1,k}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,s}e_{ij,t}e_{ik,s}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\max_{1\leq i\leq p_{2}}c_{1,i}^{2}\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j,k=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}E\left(\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}E\left(\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,s}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left|E\left(e_{ij,t}e_{ik,s}\right)\right|.

Seeing as
\mathbf{F}_{0,t}
is stationary, using Assumption
2
(ii)
(d), the above is bounded by

c_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j,k=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left|E\left(e_{ij,t}e_{ik,s}\right)\right|\leq c_{1}p_{1}p_{2}T,

whence using Lemma
5

\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}=o_{a.s.}\left(\left(p_{1}p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}\left(\log p_{1}\log^{2}p_{2}\log^{2}T\right)^{3/2+\epsilon}\right).

In turn, using Assumption
4
(ii)
, this yields

\left\|VI\right\|_{F}=o_{a.s.}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\left(\log p_{1}\log^{2}p_{2}\log^{2}T\right)^{3/2+\epsilon}\right).

The same bound holds, by symmetry, for
\left\|VI^{\prime}\right\|_{F}
.

Putting together all the above, by symmetry it follows that

(50)

\lambda_{\max}\left(II+III+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI+VI^{\prime}\right)=o_{a.s.}\left(\frac{\left(\log T\right)^{3/2+\epsilon}}{T}\right).

Finally, consider
I
in (
48
). By construction,
\lambda_{j}\left(I\right)=0
a.s. for all
j>h_{R_{1}}
; when
j\leq h_{R_{1}}
, it holds
that

\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{\log\log T}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\right)=\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}}\mathbf{R}_{1}\left(\frac{\log\log T}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\right),

seeing as
\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}=p_{2}\mathbf{I}_{h_{C_{1}}}
; using
the multiplicative version of Weyl’s inequality (see e.g. Theorem 7 in
Merikoski and
Kumar, 2004
)

(51)

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}}\mathbf{R}_{1}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\right)\lambda_{\min}\left(\left(\frac{\log\log T}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\geq c_{0}\text{ a.s.,}

having used Assumption
4
(ii)
and Lemma
7
. The
desired results now follow by combining (
51
) and (
50
),
using Weyl’s inequality.
∎

Lemma 13
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
are
satisfied. Then there exists a positive, finite constant
c_{0}
and a
triplet of random variables
\left(p_{0,0},p_{2,0},T_{0}\right)
such
that, for all
p_{1}\geq p_{0,0}
,
p_{2}\geq p_{2,0}
and
T\geq T_{0}
, it
holds that

\left(\log\log T\right)\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{M}_{C_{1}}\right)\geq c_{0}\text{, \ \ for all }j\leq h_{C_{1}}.

and, for all
\epsilon>0

\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{M}_{C_{1}}\right)=o_{a.s.}\left(\frac{\left(\log T\right)^{3/2+\epsilon}}{T}\right),\text{ \ \ for all }j>h_{C_{1}}.

Proof.

The proof is the same,
mutatis mutandis
, as that of Lemma
12
and therefore we omit it.
∎

Lemma 14
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
are
satisfied. Then it holds that

(52)

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1),

(53)

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1).

Proof.

We consider (
52
) only; the proof of (
53
) is similar.
Following the passages in the proof of Lemma
12
therein
verbatim
, it is easy to see that (
50
) becomes

\lambda_{\max}\left(II+III+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI+VI^{\prime}\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right).

Similarly, when
j\leq h_{R_{1}}
, it holds that

\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\right)\geq\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\right)\lambda_{\min}\left(\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right)\right),

recalling the identification restriction
\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}=p_{2}\mathbf{I}_{h_{C_{1}}}
. Using the multiplicative version of Weyl’s
inequality (see e.g. Theorem 7 in
Merikoski and
Kumar, 2004
)

\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}}\mathbf{R}_{1}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\right)\geq\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\right)\lambda_{\min}\left(\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right)\right).

By Assumption
4
(ii)
,
\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}/p_{1}\right)>0
. Let now
\mathbf{b}
be a nonzero
h_{R_{1}}\times 1
vector; it is immediate to see that
\mathbf{b}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{t}
is a decomposable Bernoulli shift with the same
rate as
\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{t}
. Hence, the FCLT for Bernoulli shifts
(see Theorem A.1 in
Aue
et al., 2009
) entails that

\limsup_{T\rightarrow\infty}P\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{b}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{b}\leq\epsilon^{\prime}\right)\leq P\left(\int_{0}^{1}W^{2}\left(r\right)dr\leq\epsilon^{\prime}\right),

where
\left\{W\left(r\right),0\leq r\leq 1\right\}
is a Wiener process
with variance
\mathbf{b}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{F}^{\left(a\right)}\mathbf{b}
; Assumption
1
(ii)
entails that
\mathbf{b}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{F}^{\left(a\right)}\mathbf{b>0}
. Hence, using
e.g. Theorem 1.1 in
Li (2001)
, for any
\epsilon^{\prime}\rightarrow 0^{+}

\displaystyle\limsup_{T\rightarrow\infty}P\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{b}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{b}\leq\epsilon^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\int_{0}^{1}W^{2}\left(r\right)dr\leq\epsilon^{\prime}\right)=\exp\left(-\frac{1}{8\epsilon^{\prime}}\right)=\epsilon^{\prime\prime},

and therefore

P\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{b}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{b}>\epsilon^{\prime}\right)\geq 1-\epsilon^{\prime\prime}.

In turn, this readily entails that

\lim_{T\rightarrow\infty}P\left(\lambda_{\min}\left(\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right)\right)>0\right)=1,

which implies the desired result.
∎

Lemma 15
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
are
satisfied. Then it holds that
\left\|\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}\right\|=O_{P}(1)
.

Proof.

Some arguments in the proof are the same as in the proof of Lemma
12
, to which we refer for details. It holds that

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=I+II+III+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI+VI^{\prime}.

We begin by finding bounds for
II
,
III
,
IV
,
V
, and
VI
, using
h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=h_{R_{0}}=h_{C_{1}}=1
for simplicity whenever possible. By standard
arguments, we have

\left\|II\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{2}\right)=O_{a.s.}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right).

Following the proof of Lemma
12
, it is immediate to see that
\left\|III\right\|_{F}
,
\left\|IV\right\|_{F}
,
\left\|V\right\|_{F}
, and
\left\|VI\right\|_{F}
are all
dominated by
\left\|II\right\|_{F}
. This entails that

\lambda_{\max}\left(II+III+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI+VI^{\prime}\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right),

and therefore

\lambda_{j}\left(\hat{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right),

for all
j>h_{R_{1}}
. Consider now
I
; it holds that

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=I_{a}+I_{b}+I_{b}^{\prime}+I_{c}.

It is easy to see that

\displaystyle\left\|I_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{3}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}p_{2}^{3/2}\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{T}T^{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right),

and the same holds for
\left\|I_{b}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}
.
Similarly

\displaystyle\left\|I_{c}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}p_{2}\frac{p_{2}}{T^{2}}T^{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right).

Finally, it holds that, for all
j\leq h_{R_{1}}

\lambda_{j}\left(I_{a}\right)\geq\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}}{p_{1}}\right)\lambda_{\min}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right).

Recall that Theorem
1
states that
\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}=\mathbf{I}_{h_{R_{1}}}+o_{P}(1)
, which entails that
\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}=\mathbf{I}_{h_{R_{1}}}+o_{P}\left(1\right)
. Now the same arguments as in the proof of Lemma
14
entail that
P\left(\lambda_{j}\left(I_{a}\right)>0\right)\geq 1-\epsilon
for any
\epsilon>0
, which implies the desired result.
∎

Lemma 17
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
are
satisfied. Then it holds that

\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right).

Proof.

Recall that, by construction,
\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}=p_{1}\mathbf{I}_{h_{R_{1}}}
; recall the identification restriction
\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}=p_{1}\mathbf{I}_{h_{R_{1}}}
; and finally, recall
(
64
), viz.

\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}=\mathbf{I}_{h_{R_{1}}}+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right).

Then, using Theorem
1
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbf{I}_{p_{1}}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}-\left(\mathbf{I}_{p_{1}}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\left(\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}}\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{\prime}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{p_{1}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}+\frac{1}{p_{1}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right\|_{F}^{2}+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right).

∎

.

Lemma 19
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
are
satisfied. Then there exists a positive constant
c_{0}
such that

\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{M}_{X}^{R_{1},\perp}\right)=c_{0}+o_{P}\left(1\right)\text{,}

for all
j\leq h_{R_{1}}
, and

\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{M}_{X}^{R_{1},\perp}\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right),

for all
j>h_{R_{1}}
.

Proof.

Some arguments are repetitive and similar to the proof of Lemma
12
, and therefore we omit them when possible for brevity. By construction

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{X}^{R_{1},\perp}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I+II+III+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI+VI^{\prime}.

We begin by noting that we can always write

(54)

\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}=\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right).

We will work under the restrictions
h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=1
, for simplicity
and with no loss of generality, and, when possible,
h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=1
.
We begin by studying

\displaystyle\left\|I\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(T^{2}\right)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}p_{2}\frac{1}{T^{2}}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right),

by virtue of (
54
) and Lemma
18
. Using the fact that

\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}
is symmetric and idempotent, we have

\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}=\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{2}=\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp};

hence

\displaystyle III

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle III_{a}+III_{b}.

Seeing as

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}=\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right),

we have (denoting the element in position
j
,
h
of
\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}

as
c_{\perp,hj}
)

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\lambda_{\max}\left(E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\leq\left(\max_{1\leq h,j\leq p_{2}}\left(c_{\perp,hj}\right)^{2}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\max_{1\leq i\leq p_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j,h=1}^{p_{2}}\left|E\left(e_{ij,t}e_{kh,t}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}Tp_{2},

and, after some algebra

\displaystyle E\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}\sum_{i,j=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{h_{1},h_{2},h_{3},h_{4}=1}^{p_{2}}\left|\cov\left(e_{ih_{1},t}e_{jh_{2},t},e_{ih_{3},s}e_{jh_{4},s}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}p_{1}^{2}Tp_{2}^{3},

by Assumption
3
(iii)
, whence ultimately

\left\|III_{a}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right).

Also, by similar calculations as above, it is not hard to see that
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\left(p_{1}p_{2}T\right)
, and therefore

\displaystyle\left\|III_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}p_{2}T\frac{1}{T}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right),

whence ultimately

(55)

\left\|III\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right).

Further, again exploiting idempotency and (
54
)

\displaystyle\left\|IV\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}\frac{1}{T}\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right),

having used Lemma
11
. Similarly

\displaystyle\left\|V\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}\frac{1}{T}T=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right).

Finally, we have

\displaystyle VI

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle VI_{a}+VI_{b}.

It holds that

\displaystyle E\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}c_{\perp,hj}e_{ij,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{p_{2}}c_{\perp,hj_{1}}c_{\perp,hj_{2}}E\left(e_{ij_{1},t}e_{ij_{2},s}\right)E\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,s}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\max_{1\leq h,j\leq p_{2}}c_{\perp,hj}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{p_{2}}\left|E\left(e_{ij_{1},t}e_{ij_{2},s}\right)\right|\left|E\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,s}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}\left(\max_{1\leq h,j\leq p_{2}}c_{\perp,hj}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{p_{2}}\left|E\left(e_{ij_{1},t}e_{ij_{2},s}\right)\right|\leq c_{1}p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T,

and therefore we have

\displaystyle\left\|VI_{a}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right).

Also

\displaystyle\left\|VI_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}\frac{1}{T}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}T^{3/2}}\right),

so that ultimately

\left\|VI\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right).

Putting all together, it follows that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}\left(I+III+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI+VI^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right).

Consider now
II
; by construction,
\lambda_{j}\left(II\right)=0

whenever
j>h_{R_{1}}
. Further

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle II_{a}+II_{b}.

Note that, by standard algebra

\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}=c_{0}p_{2}^{2},

and therefore

\displaystyle\left\|II_{a}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}E\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle II_{a,1}+II_{a,2}.

Using Assumptions
2
and
4
, it is easy to see via tedious
but elementary passages that
\lambda_{j}\left(II_{a,1}\right)\geq c_{0}
, and
\lambda_{\max}\left(II_{a,2}\right)=O_{P}\left(T^{-1/2}\right)
. Moreover

\displaystyle\left\|II_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(T\right)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}p_{2}\frac{1}{T}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right).

Putting all together, it follows that

\lambda_{j}\left(II\right)=c_{0}+o_{P}(1),

for all
j\leq h_{R_{1}}
. The desired result now follows from Weyl’s
inequality.
∎

Lemma 20
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
are
satisfied. Then there exists a positive constant
c_{0}
such that

\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{M}_{C_{1},\perp}\right)=c_{0}+o_{P}\left(1\right)\text{,}

for all
j\leq h_{C_{0}}
, and

\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{M}_{C_{1},\perp}\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right),

for all
j>h_{C_{0}}
.

Proof.

The proof is the same as the proof of Lemma
19
,
mutatis mutandis
.
∎

Lemma 21
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
hold. Then it
holds that

(56)

\displaystyle\sigma_{\max}\left[\frac{1}{p_{1}}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{0}}\right)\right]

(57)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right),

and

(58)

\displaystyle\sigma_{\max}\left[\frac{1}{p_{2}}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}-\mathbf{C}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{0}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{p_{1}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right).

Proof.

We only show (
56
); the proof of (
57
) is essentially the
same. It holds that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{0}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I+II+III+IV+V+VI+VII+VIII+IX+X+XI.

We have

\displaystyle\left\|I\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{2}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\frac{1}{T}p_{1}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}\frac{1}{T^{2}}T^{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T^{2}}\right).

Note now that

\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)=\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{I}_{p_{2}}-\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}=\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\left[\mathbf{C}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right],

and since
\mathbf{C}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}
is an idempotent matrix with
h_{C_{1}}
nonzero
eigenvalues, we have

(59)

\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\right\|_{F}=O\left(\frac{1}{T}\right),

and similarly

(60)

\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\right\|_{F}=O\left(\frac{1}{T}\right).

Hence we have

\displaystyle\left\|II\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{2}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}p_{2}\frac{1}{T}T=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}T}\right),

and the same rate holds for
III
; further

\displaystyle\left\|IV\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{2}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}^{5/2}p_{2}\frac{1}{T^{2}}T=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}T^{2}}\right).

We now study
V
, using the decomposition

\displaystyle V

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{0}}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{0}}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{0}}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{0}}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{0}}\right)\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}+V_{f}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle V_{a}+V_{b}+V_{c}+V_{d}+V_{e}+V_{f},

where
V_{f}
is a remainder which can be shown to be dominated by the other
terms. We begin with
V_{a}
, and note that

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{R}_{0}+\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right]\mathbf{R}_{0};

the element in position
1\leq k\leq p_{1}
of the vector (recall we are
assuming only one factor)
\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{R}_{0}
is

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{u=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{p_{2}}p_{uikh\ell}=r_{0,u}r_{\perp,ik}c_{\perp,h\ell}E\left(e_{ih,t}e_{u\ell,t}\right),

where
p_{uikh\ell}=r_{0,u}r_{\perp,ik}c_{\perp,h\ell}
, and therefore

\displaystyle\sigma_{\max}\left(\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{R}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{k=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{u=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{p_{2}}\left|p_{uikh\ell}\right|\left|E\left(e_{ih,t}e_{u\ell,t}\right)\right|\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\max_{1\leq k\leq p_{1}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{u=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{p_{2}}\left|p_{uikh\ell}\right|\left|E\left(e_{ih,t}e_{u\ell,t}\right)\right|\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}\left(p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}\left(p_{1}p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}\leq c_{1}p_{1}^{3/2}p_{2}T.

Also

\displaystyle E\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right]\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h_{1},h_{2},h_{3},h_{4}=1}^{p_{2}}p_{i_{1}i_{2}kh_{1}h_{2}}p_{i_{3}i_{4}kh_{3}h_{4}}\cov\left(e_{i_{1}h_{1},t}e_{i_{2}h_{2},t},e_{i_{3}h_{3},s}e_{i_{4}h_{4},s}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}\sum_{k=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h_{1},h_{2},h_{3},h_{4}=1}^{p_{2}}\left|\cov\left(e_{i_{1}h_{1},t}e_{i_{2}h_{2},t},e_{i_{3}h_{3},s}e_{i_{4}h_{4},s}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}p_{1}^{4}p_{2}^{3}T,

whence

\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right]\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{3/2}T^{1/2}\right).

Therefore we have

\sigma_{\max}\left(V_{a}\right)=O\left(\frac{p_{1}^{3/2}p_{2}T}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{3/2}T^{1/2}}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\right)=O\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right).

We now note that

\sigma_{\max}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\right)=O_{P}\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T\right)+O_{P}\left(p_{1}p_{2}^{3/2}T^{1/2}\right).

Indeed, the element in position
1\leq i\leq p_{1}
of
\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}
is given by

\sum_{k=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j,h=1}^{p_{2}}r_{0,k}c_{\perp,jh}e_{ij,t}e_{kh,t};

hence

\displaystyle\sigma_{\max}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i,k=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j,h=1}^{p_{2}}\left|r_{0,k}c_{\perp,jh}\right|\left|E\left(e_{ij,t}e_{kh,t}\right)\right|\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\max_{1\leq i\leq p_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j,h=1}^{p_{2}}\left|r_{0,k}c_{\perp,jh}\right|\left|E\left(e_{ij,t}e_{kh,t}\right)\right|\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}\left(p_{1}p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}\left(p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}\leq c_{1}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T;

also

\displaystyle E\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\right)\right]\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{h_{1},h_{2}=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h_{1},h_{2},h_{3},h_{4}=1}^{p_{2}}\left|\cov\left(e_{ih_{1},t}e_{k_{1}h_{2},t},e_{ih_{3},s}e_{k_{2}h_{4},s}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{3}T,

whence

\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\right)\right]\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(p_{1}p_{2}^{3/2}T^{1/2}\right).

We therefore have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{0}}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{0}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\sigma_{\max}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\frac{1}{T}\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T+p_{1}p_{2}^{3/2}T^{1/2}\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{3/2}p_{2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right).

Continuing with
V_{c}
, the same passages as in the above yield

\displaystyle\left\|V_{c}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left(p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}\left(p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T\right)^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right),

and the same can be shown for
V_{d}
and (with a different, but still
dominated, rate)
V_{e}
. We now turn to
VI
; omitting some passages
already considered above, we have

\displaystyle\left\|VI\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}+r_{p_{1}p_{2}T},

where
r_{p_{1}p_{2}T}
is a (dominated) remainder term, and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\frac{1}{T}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}\frac{1}{T}Tp_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{2}}\right);

the same can be shown for
VII
. Also

\displaystyle\left\|VIII\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\frac{1}{T}p_{1}^{1/2}\frac{1}{T}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}Tp_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T^{2}}\right),

and the same holds for
IX
. Also repeating the passages above, we receive

\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle\left\|X\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}+r_{p_{1}p_{2}T}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right),

with
r_{p_{1}p_{2}T}^{\prime}
a (dominated) remainder. Finally

\displaystyle\left\|XI\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}+r_{p_{1}p_{2}T}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}p_{2}T^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right),

with, as usual,
r_{p_{1}p_{2}T}^{\prime\prime}
a (dominated) remainder.
The desired result now follows from putting all together.
∎

Lemma 22
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
are
satisfied. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right),

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{{}_{1,\perp}}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right),

Proof.

The proof follows from a minor adaptation of the proof of Lemma
17
.
∎

Lemma 23
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
hold. Let
\mathbf{a}
be a
p_{1}\times 1
vector with
\left\|\mathbf{a}\right\|=O\left(p_{1}^{1/2}\right)
, and
\mathbf{b}
be a
p_{2}\times 1
vector with
\left\|\mathbf{b}\right\|=O\left(p_{2}^{1/2}\right)
. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\sigma_{\max}\left[\frac{1}{p_{1}}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{0}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{\left(p_{1}p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}T^{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{3/2}T^{3/2}}\right),

and

\displaystyle\sigma_{\max}\left[\frac{1}{p_{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{0}-\mathbf{C}_{0}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{0}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{\left(p_{1}p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{p_{1}T^{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{p_{1}^{2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{p_{1}^{3/2}T^{3/2}}\right).

Proof.

The proof is essentially the same as that of Lemma
21
,
mutatis mutandis
, using (also) the fact that

\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right),

and

\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right).

and the same (with the rates in Lemma
22
) for
\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}
.
∎

Lemma 24
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
are satisfied.
Then it holds that

\frac{1}{T}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1\wedge 2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right).

Proof.

We prove the lemma for the case
h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=1
,
with no loss of generality. We use the same arguments as in the proof of
Theorem
3
, obtaining

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{\perp}^{\prime}\right)\left(\left(\mathbf{C}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{\perp}^{\prime}\right)\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{\perp}^{\prime}\right)\left(\left(\mathbf{C}_{1}-\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right)\left(\mathbf{C}_{1}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{1}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Ve\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\right)\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I+II+III+IV+V.

Recall (
70
); it follows that

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\left(\mathbf{C}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle=
