\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{2}}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}p_{2}^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}p_{1}^{1/2}T

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{T}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\left(\frac{T}{p_{1}}\right)^{1/2}\right),

and, by the same passages

II=O_{P}\left(\frac{T}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\left(\frac{T}{p_{2}}\right)^{1/2}\right),

and
III
is dominated by
I
and
II
; also

\displaystyle IV

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\mathbf{C}_{1}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right)\mathbf{R}_{1}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{2}}p_{2}^{1/2}\frac{1}{T}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}\frac{1}{T}p_{1}^{1/2}T^{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right).

Finally

\displaystyle V

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right)\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Ve\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\right)\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Ve\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\right)\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\left(\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Ve\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\right)\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\left(\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right)\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Ve\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\right)\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\otimes\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Ve\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\right)\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right)\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Ve\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\right)\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle V_{a}+V_{b}+V_{c}+V_{d}.

Noting that

\displaystyle E\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}r_{\perp,ij}c_{\perp,h\ell}e_{jh,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h_{1},h_{2}=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}r_{\perp,ij_{1}}r_{\perp,ij_{2}}c_{\perp,h_{1}\ell}c_{\perp,h_{2}\ell}E\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,s}\right)E\left(e_{j_{1}h_{1},t}e_{j_{2}h_{2},s}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h_{1},h_{2}=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left(E\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{2}\right)E\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,s}^{2}\right)\right)^{1/2}\left|E\left(e_{j_{1}h_{1},t}e_{j_{2}h_{2},s}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}T\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h_{1},h_{2}=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left|E\left(e_{ij,t}e_{hk,s}\right)\right|\leq c_{2}\left(p_{1}p_{2}T\right)^{2},

it immediately follows that

\left\|V_{a}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(p_{1}p_{2}T\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{T}{\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{1/2}}\right).

Similarly, seeing as

\displaystyle E\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}c_{\perp,hj}^{s}e_{ij,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right)^{2}\leq c_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{j,k=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left(E\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{2}\right)E\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,s}^{2}\right)\right)^{1/2}\left|E\left(e_{ij,t}e_{ik,s}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2},

we have

\displaystyle\left\|V_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{2}}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}\frac{1}{T}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{1/2}}\right).

The same holds for
V_{c}
, and
V_{d}
is clearly dominated by
V_{b}
and
V_{c}
. Hence

\left\|V\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{T}{\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{1/2}}\right).

∎

Lemma 25
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
are
satisfied. Then there exists a positive constant
c_{0}
such that

\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{M}_{X}^{R_{1},PE}\right)=c_{0}+o_{P}\left(1\right)\text{,}

for all
j\leq h_{R_{1}}
, and

\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{M}_{X}^{R_{1},PE}\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1\wedge 2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right),

for all
j>h_{R_{1}}
.

Proof.

The proof repeats several arguments already discussed above, which are
therefore omitted. Note that

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{X}^{R_{1},PE}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle=I+II+III+III^{\prime}+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI.

We have already studied terms
II+III+III^{\prime}
in the proof of Lemma
2
, with

II+III+III^{\prime}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right).

Further, write

(61)

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}+\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}-\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\left(\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{\prime}+

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}-\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\left(\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}-\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}-\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{\prime}.

Then we have

\displaystyle IV

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}=IV_{a}+IV_{b};

using (
61
), we can write

IV_{a}=\sum_{i=1}^{7}IV_{a,i}.

Following the passages in the above, it holds that

\displaystyle\left\|IV_{a,1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}\left(p_{1}p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)\right);

\displaystyle\left\|IV_{a,2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}-\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{1}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)\right);

\displaystyle\left\|IV_{a,3}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{3/2}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{1\wedge 2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right),

having used (
18
) in the last set of equations. By the same
token, it can be shown that
IV_{a,4}-IV_{a,7}
are all dominated by
IV_{a,1}-IV_{a,3}
. Similarly, using (
61
), we can write

IV_{b}=\sum_{i=1}^{7}IV_{b,i}.

It holds that

\displaystyle\left\|IV_{b,1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)p_{2}^{1/2}\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{T}\left(p_{1}p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{5/2}}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)\right);

\displaystyle\left\|IV_{b,2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}-\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{5/2}}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{1}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)\right).

We now study, along similar lines as the proof of Lemma
24

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbf{E}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\mathbf{C}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\right)\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\otimes\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\right)\left(\left(\mathbf{C}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\right)\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\right)^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\mathbf{E}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle a+b+c+d+e.

By using the same arguments as in the above we have

\displaystyle\left\|a\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}}p_{1}^{3/2}p_{2}^{3/2}\left(p_{1}p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{T^{1/2}}{p_{1}p_{2}^{1/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{p_{1}^{1/2}}\right);

\displaystyle\left\|b\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}}p_{1}p_{2}\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(p_{1}p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{T^{1/2}}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}}\right);

also, it follows by the same logic that
\left\|c\right\|_{F}
is
dominated by the other two terms;

\displaystyle\left\|d\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right)\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}}p_{2}^{1/2}\frac{1}{T}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}\frac{1}{T}p_{1}^{1/2}\left(p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}\right)^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right).

Finally

\left\|e\right\|_{F}\leq\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}+r_{p_{1}p_{2}T},

where
r_{p_{1}p_{2}T}
is a dominated remainder, and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h_{1},h_{2}=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{u_{1},u_{2}=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}=1}^{p_{2}}r_{\perp,h_{1}\ell_{1}}r_{\perp,h_{2}\ell_{2}}r_{0,\ell_{1}}r_{0,\ell_{2}}c_{\perp,k_{1}u_{1}}c_{\perp,k_{2}u_{2}}c_{0,u_{1}}c_{0,u_{2}}e_{h_{1}k_{1},t}e_{h_{2}k_{2},t}e_{ij,t}e_{ij,s},

which can be shown to be
O_{P}\left(p_{1}^{4}p_{2}^{4}T\right)
; putting
all together, it follows that

\left\|e\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(T^{1/2}\right).

Hence it follows that

\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(T^{1/2}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{p_{1}^{1/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}}\right),

and therefore

\displaystyle\left\|IV_{b,3}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{T^{5/2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}+\frac{1}{p_{1\wedge 2}T^{1/2}}\right),

and again by the same logic, it can be shown that
IV_{b,4}-IV_{b,7}
are
all dominated by
IV_{b,1}-IV_{b,3}
. By the same logic

V=\sum_{i=1}^{7}V_{i},

with

\displaystyle\left\|V_{1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{2}p_{1}^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)T

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)\right),

\displaystyle\left\|V_{2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}-\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{1}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)\right),

\displaystyle\left\|V_{3}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{1\wedge 2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{1\wedge 2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)\right),

again by Lemma
24
; similarly, it can be shown that
V_{4}-V_{7}
are
all dominated by
V_{1}-V_{3}
. Finally we write

VI=\sum_{i=1}^{7}VI_{i}.

It holds that

\displaystyle\left\|VI_{1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right\|_{F}^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{2}p_{2}p_{1}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right)T

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right)\right),

and similarly

\left\|VI_{2}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}T}\right)\right),

and

\displaystyle\left\|VI_{3}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{2}p_{2}p_{1}T\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{1\wedge 2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{1\wedge 2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)^{2}\right);

again, it can be shown by the same logic that
VI_{4}-VI_{7}
are all
dominated by
VI_{1}-VI_{3}
. The desired result now follows from the same
logic as in the previous proofs.
∎

Lemma 26
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
are
satisfied. Then there exists a positive constant
c_{0}
such that

\lambda_{j}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{C_{1}}\right)=c_{0}+o_{P}(1)\text{,}

for all
j\leq h_{C_{1}}
, and

\lambda_{j}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{C_{1}}\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1\wedge 2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right),

for all
j>h_{C_{1}}
.

Proof.

The proof is the same as the proof of Lemma
25
,
mutatis mutandis
.
∎

Lemma 27
.

We assume that Assumptions
1
-
5
are
satisfied. Then it holds that

\left\|\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|=O_{P}(1),\text{ \ \ and \ \ }\left\|\widetilde{\Lambda}_{C_{1}}^{-1}\right\|=O_{P}(1).

Proof.

The proof follows from Lemmas
25
and
26
,
in the same way as the proof of Lemma
15
.
∎

Appendix B
Proofs

Henceforth, we will use the following notation:
\log\left(x\right)
is
the natural log of
x
;

Proof of Theorem
1
.

We begin by studying the estimator of
\mathbf{R}_{1}
. By construction, it holds that

\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}=\mathbf{M}_{R_{1}}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1},

where recall that, by Lemma
14
,
\left\|\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|=O_{P}(1)
. Hence

(62)

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle=I+II+III+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI+VI^{\prime}.

Define

(63)

\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}=\frac{1}{p_{1}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\frac{\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}}{p_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}=\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}}{p_{1}}\right)\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1};

then it is immediate to see that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)

having used the identification restriction
\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}=p_{2}\mathbf{I}_{h_{C_{1}}}
, and the facts that
\left\|\mathbf{R}\right\|_{F}=O\left(p_{1}^{1/2}\right)
,
\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}=p_{1}^{1/2}\
by construction, and
\left\|\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}(1)
, and (
40
) in
Lemma
6
. Further (using
h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=h_{R_{0}}=h_{C_{0}}=1
)

\left\|II\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{2}\right)=O_{P}(1)\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T},

having used Assumption
4
, and Lemmas
8
and
14
. Consider now

\left\|III\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\lambda_{\max}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right),

by Lemma
10
. Similarly

\displaystyle\left\|IV\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\right|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(T\right)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T}\right),

having used Assumption
4
and Lemma
9
. The same holds for

\left\|IV^{\prime}\right\|_{F}
. By the same token

\displaystyle\left\|V\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T\right)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right),

by Assumption
4
, the fact that
\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T\right)
, and Lemma
14
; and the same also
holds for
\left\|V^{\prime}\right\|_{F}
. Finally, using the same
arguments as above, it holds that

\displaystyle\left\|VI\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}\right)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right),

and the same holds for
\left\|VI^{\prime}\right\|_{F}
. Then,
putting all together, it follows that

\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T}\right).

We conclude the proof by showing that
\left\|\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}(1)
. Recall that, by
construction,
\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}=p_{1}\mathbf{I}_{h_{R_{1}}}
; recall also the identification restriction
\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}=p_{1}\mathbf{I}_{h_{R_{1}}}
; hence

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}_{h_{R_{1}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}_{1}}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}^{\prime}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\right)\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)+\frac{1}{p_{1}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}+I+I^{\prime}+II.

Clearly

\left\|I\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right);

the same holds for
\left\|I^{\prime}\right\|_{F}
, and, by the
same token,
\left\|II\right\|_{F}
is dominated. Hence

(64)

\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}=\mathbf{I}_{h_{R_{1}}}+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right).

Thus, as
\min\left\{p_{1},p_{2},T\right\}\rightarrow\infty
,
\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}
is an orthogonal matrix, and therefore
\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}=\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}^{\prime}+o_{P}(1)
. Now
\left\|\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}(1)
follows immediately.

We now turn to studyingt the estimator of
\mathbf{C}_{1}
. Observing that

\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}=\mathbf{M}_{C_{1}}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1},

and that, by Lemma
14
,
\left\|\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1}\right\|=O_{P}(1)
, it holds that

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1}+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1}+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle=I+II+III+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI+VI^{\prime}.

Letting

(65)

\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}=\frac{1}{p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\frac{\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}}{p_{1}}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1}=\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}}{p_{2}}\right)\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{-1},

the proof proceeds as above,
mutatis
mutandis
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
1
.

Recall that

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{1,t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}

\displaystyle=I+II+III.

We will use the decompositions

\mathbf{R}_{1}=\mathbf{R}_{1}\pm\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1},\text{ \ \ and \ \ }\mathbf{C}_{1}=\mathbf{C}_{1}\pm\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{-1}.

Consider
I
; it holds that

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=\frac{\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}}{p_{1}}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\frac{\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}}{p_{2}}-\frac{\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\right)}{p_{1}}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\frac{\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}}{p_{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}}{p_{1}}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\frac{\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{-1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}}{p_{2}}+\frac{\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\right)}{p_{1}}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\frac{\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{-1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}}{p_{2}}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}-I_{a}-I_{b}+I_{c}.

By (
39
) in Lemma
6
, it immediately follows that
\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(T^{1/2}\right)
. Hence

\left\|I_{a}\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}}{p_{1}}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}\left\|\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(T^{-1/2}\right),

having used Theorem
1
; similarly

\left\|I_{b}\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{-1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}}{p_{1}}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}\left\|\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(T^{-1/2}\right),

by Theorem
1
. By the same token, it is easy to see that
\left\|I_{c}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(T^{-3/2}\right)
. Further

\left\|II\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(1\right),

which is a consequence of the fact that, by Assumption
2
,
\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}(1)
. Finally

\displaystyle\left\|III\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left(\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}+\left\|\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle+\left\|\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left(\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)\right\|_{F}+\left\|\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right).

This follows because

\displaystyle E\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=E\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}r_{i}c_{j}e_{ij,t}\right)^{2}\leq\left(\max_{1\leq i\leq p_{1}}r_{i}^{2}\right)\left(\max_{1\leq i\leq p_{2}}c_{i}^{2}\right)\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=1}^{p_{2}}\left|E\left(e_{ij,t}e_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime},t}\right)\right|\leq c_{0}p_{1}p_{2},

by Assumption
3
(ii)
(e); also

\displaystyle E\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=E\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}c_{j}e_{ij,t}\right)^{2}\right)\leq\left(\max_{1\leq i\leq p_{2}}c_{i}^{2}\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=1}^{p_{2}}\left|E\left(e_{ij,t}e_{ij^{\prime},t}\right)\right|\leq c_{0}p_{1}p_{2},

by Assumption
3
(ii)
(c); further, we have
E\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\leq c_{0}p_{1}p_{2}
,
by Assumption
3
(ii)
(b) and the same arguments as above;
and, finally, we also have
E\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{F}^{2}

=

\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}E\left(e_{ij,t}^{2}\right)

\leq

c_{0}p_{1}p_{2}
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
2
.

We study the estimator of
\mathbf{R}_{1}
first. Some of the arguments in the proof are
based on repeating some of the passages above, and we therefore omit them
for brevity. It holds that

(66)

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}_{1}}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle=I+II+III+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI+VI^{\prime}.

We have

I=\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}^{\dagger},

having defined

\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}=\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}}{p_{2}}\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}}{p_{2}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\frac{\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}}{p_{1}}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}.

Lemma
15
entails that
\left\|\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}\right\|=O_{P}(1)
; hence

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{H}_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}}{p_{2}}\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}}{p_{2}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\frac{\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}}{p_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\frac{\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}}{p_{2}}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\frac{\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}}{p_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}(1).

Further, recalling that
h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=h_{R_{0}}=h_{C_{0}}=1

\displaystyle\left\|II\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{2}\right)\left\|\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{3/2}p_{2}^{2}O_{P}\left(T\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T}\right).

Moreover,

\displaystyle III

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=III_{a}+III_{b}+III_{b}^{\prime}+III_{c}.

Under
h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=h_{R_{0}}=h_{C_{0}}=1
,
\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}
is a random sign under
our identification restrictions, so we will omit it; it holds that

\displaystyle\left\|III_{a}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\right\|_{F}\left\|\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq p_{1}^{1/2}\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right).

Also

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\leq\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\right).

It holds that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\max_{1\leq h\leq p_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{p_{1}}\left|E\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}c_{j}e_{hj,t}\right)\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}c_{j}e_{kj,t}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq c_{0}\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\max_{1\leq h\leq p_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h,j=1}^{p_{2}}\left|E\left(e_{hj,t}e_{kj,t}\right)\right|\leq c_{1}\frac{1}{p_{2}T};

also

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left(\sum_{i,j=1}^{p_{1}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}c_{h}\left(e_{ih,t}-E\left(e_{ih,t}\right)\right)\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{p_{2}}c_{k}\left(e_{ik,t}-E\left(e_{ik,t}\right)\right)\right)\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2},

and

\displaystyle E\sum_{i,j=1}^{p_{1}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}c_{h}\left(e_{ih,t}-E\left(e_{ih,t}\right)\right)\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{p_{2}}c_{k}\left(e_{ik,t}-E\left(e_{ik,t}\right)\right)\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\max_{1\leq h\leq p_{2}}\left|c_{h}\right|\right)^{4}\sum_{i,j=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h_{1},h_{2},h_{3},h_{4}=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left|\cov\left(e_{ih_{1},t}e_{jh_{2},t},e_{ih_{3},s}e_{jh_{4},s}\right)\right|\leq c_{0}p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{3}T,

whence

\left\|\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-E\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right).

Hence

\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right),

which in turn entails that

\left\|III_{a}\right\|_{F}=O\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right).

Following the proof of Lemma C.5 in
He
et al. (2023)
, it can be shown that
III_{b}
and
III_{c}
are both dominated by
III_{a}
. We also have (recall
that we are assuming
h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=1
, and that therefore
\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}
is
a random sign)

\displaystyle IV

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\left(\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=IV_{a}+IV_{b}.

Consider now

\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}=\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\left|\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}c_{h}e_{ih,t}\right|^{2}\right)^{1/2},

with

\displaystyle E\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\left|\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}c_{h}e_{ih,t}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=E\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h,k=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}c_{h}c_{k}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,s}e_{ih,t}e_{ik,s}=\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h,k=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}c_{h}c_{k}E\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,s}\right)E\left(e_{ih,t}e_{ik,s}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq h\leq p_{2}}c_{h}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h,k=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left|E\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,s}\right)\right|\left|E\left(e_{ih,t}e_{ik,s}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq c_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h,k=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left|E\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{2}\right)E\left(\mathbf{F}_{1,s}^{2}\right)\right|^{1/2}\left|E\left(e_{ih,t}e_{ik,s}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq c_{1}T\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{h,k=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left|E\left(e_{ih,t}e_{ik,s}\right)\right|\leq c_{2}p_{1}p_{2}T^{2},

whence

(67)

\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T\right).

Hence it follows that

\left\|IV_{a}\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right);

also

\displaystyle\left\|IV_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T^{2}}\right);

thus

\left\|IV\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T^{2}}\right).

Similarly, it is not hard to see that

\displaystyle\left\|V\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right|\left\|\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{3/2}p_{2}^{2}T=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T^{2}}\right),

and

\displaystyle\left\|VI\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\dagger}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\left(\Lambda_{R_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\left(p_{1}p_{2}T\right)^{1/2}+\frac{\left(p_{1}T\right)^{1/2}p_{2}}{T}\right)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}p_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T^{5/2}}\right).

The desired result now follows.

The proof of the other result follows from the same arguments as that of
Lemma
12
, upon defining

\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\dagger}=\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}}{p_{1}}\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}}{p_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\frac{\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\dagger}}{p_{2}}\left(\Lambda_{C_{1}}^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}.

∎

Proof of Theorem
2
.

Some arguments are similar to the proof of Lemma
19
, and we
therefore omit them when possible. We begin by showing (
14
); by
definition, it holds that

\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}=\mathbf{M}_{X}^{R_{1},\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1},

whence

(68)

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I+II+III+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI+VI^{\prime}.

We begin by noting that, by Lemma
19
,
\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{R_{1}}=O_{P}(1)
. Consider
II
first

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle II_{a}+II_{b},

where we have defined

\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}=\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\frac{\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{C}_{0}}{p_{2}^{2}}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\right)\frac{\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}}{p_{1}}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

By similar arguments as in the above, it is easy to see that

\left\|\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right\|_{F}\leq\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)\left(\frac{\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}}{p_{2}}\right)^{2}\frac{\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}}{p_{1}}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}(1).

Further

\displaystyle\left\|II_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}p_{1}^{1/2}Tp_{2}\frac{1}{T}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}T}\right),

so that ultimately

II=\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}T}\right).

We also note that Assumption
4
(ii)
(b)
\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\neq 0
, together with Assumption
2
(ii)
entails that
\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}
has full rank
h_{R_{1}}
. We now consider the other terms, starting from

I=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1},

having used (
54
) in the first passage, whence

\displaystyle\left\|I\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)\frac{\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}^{2}}{p_{2}^{2}}\frac{\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}}{p_{1}}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle p_{1}^{1/2}O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}T}\right).

Similarly, using (
55
)

(69)

\displaystyle\left\|III\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)\right)p_{1}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right).

We now study (explicitly considering the case
h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=1
for simplicity)

\displaystyle IV

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle IV_{a}+IV_{b},

again having used (
54
). Hence, using the fact that (as can be
verified with a similar logic as above)

\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T\right),

it holds that

\displaystyle\left\|IV_{a}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\frac{\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}}{p_{1}}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\frac{\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}}{p_{2}^{2}}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{T}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T\frac{1}{p_{2}^{3/2}T}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right),

and

\displaystyle\left\|IV_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\frac{\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}}{p_{1}}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\frac{\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}^{2}}{p_{2}^{2}}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{T}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T\frac{1}{p_{2}^{3/2}T^{2}}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}T^{2}}\right),

by Lemma
11
, whence

\left\|IV\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right).

By the same token

V=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1},

whence

\displaystyle\left\|V\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}\frac{\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}}{p_{2}^{2}}\frac{\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}}{p_{1}}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)p_{1}^{1/2}\frac{1}{p_{2}T}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}T}\right).

Also

\displaystyle VI

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle VI_{a}+VI_{b}.

Using the fact that

\displaystyle E\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{p_{2}}c_{\perp,jh}e_{ih,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right)^{2}\leq c_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h_{1},h_{2}=1}^{p_{2}}\left|E\left(e_{ih_{1},t}e_{ih_{2},s}\right)\right|\left|E\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,s}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h_{1},h_{2}=1}^{p_{2}}\left|E\left(e_{ih_{1},t}e_{ih_{2},s}\right)\right|\leq O\left(p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T\right),

we have

\displaystyle\left\|VI_{a}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right);

also

\displaystyle\left\|VI_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}\frac{1}{T}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}T^{3/2}}\right),

having used the fact that

\displaystyle E\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}e_{ij,t}\right)^{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left|E\left(\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{0,s}\right)\right|\left|E\left(e_{ij,t}e_{ij,s}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left|E\left(e_{ij,t}e_{ij,s}\right)\right|\leq c_{1}p_{1}p_{2}T.

Hence

\left\|VI\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right).

The desired result now follows from putting everything together. As far as
the invertibility of
\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}
is concerned, it follows from
similar arguments as in the proof of Theorem
1
.

The proof of (
15
) is similar, upon noting

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{s}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\Lambda_{C_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\Lambda_{C_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\Lambda_{C_{0}}^{-1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\Lambda_{C_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\Lambda_{C_{0}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\Lambda_{C_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\Lambda_{C_{0}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\Lambda_{C_{0}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\Lambda_{C_{0}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I+II+III+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI+VI^{\prime}.

and defining

\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}=\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\frac{\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}}{p_{1}}\frac{\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{0}}{p_{1}}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right)\frac{\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}}{p_{2}}\Lambda_{C_{0}}^{-1},

\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}=\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\frac{\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}}{p_{2}}\frac{\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{0}}{p_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}^{\prime}\right)\frac{\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}}{p_{1}}\Lambda_{R_{0}}^{-1}.
