Proof of Theorem
3
.

Let

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{D}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\right)^{-1}\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right)^{-1},

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{N}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\right)\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right);

note that, by standard algebra

(70)

\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{2}}\right),

and consider the decompositions

\mathbf{R}_{0}=\mathbf{R}_{0}\pm\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1},\text{ \ \ and \ \ }\mathbf{C}_{0}=\mathbf{C}_{0}\pm\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}.

We are now ready to start the proof. It holds that

(71)

\displaystyle\Ve\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\right)\left(\mathbf{C}_{0}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{0}\right)\Ve\mathbf{F}_{0,t}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\right)\left(\mathbf{C}_{1}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{1}\right)\Ve\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\right)\Ve\mathbf{E}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I+II+III.

Note

(72)

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\right)\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\right)\left(\left(\mathbf{C}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\right)\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\left.\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbf{N}}\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right)^{\prime}\right)\left(\left(\mathbf{C}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\times\Ve\mathbf{F}_{0,t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I_{a}+I_{b}+I_{c}+I_{d}.

By standard algebraic manipulations

I_{a}=\Ve\left(\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right).

Further

\displaystyle\left\|I_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\left(\mathbf{C}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\right)\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\right\|_{F}\left\|\Ve\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\left(\mathbf{C}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\right)\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\right\|_{F}\left\|\Ve\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\sigma_{\max}\left[\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\left(\mathbf{C}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\times\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Ve\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{2}}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}\left[\left(O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{p_{1}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)\right)\right]p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}p_{1}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}T}\right),

having used (
70
), Lemma
21
and Theorem
2
.
Similarly we can show that

\left\|I_{c}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right),

and by the same logic, it can be shown that
\left\|I_{d}\right\|_{F}
is dominated. Further, using (
54
) and the similar result
\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}=\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right)
, it holds that

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\right)\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\right)\Ve\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{C}_{1}\right)\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}\mathbf{R}_{1}\right)\right)\Ve\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right)\mathbf{C}_{1}\right)\otimes\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\prime}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right)\mathbf{R}_{1}\right)\right)\Ve\mathbf{F}_{1,t}

whence

\displaystyle\left\|II\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}-\mathbf{R}_{1,\perp}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Ve\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{2}}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}\frac{1}{T}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}\frac{1}{T}T^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}T^{3/2}}\right),

having used (
39
) in Lemma
6
and Lemmas
17

and
18
. Finally, using the same logic as in the above, it can be
shown that (modulo some higher order terms)

\displaystyle\left\|III\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{D}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,\perp}^{s}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{s}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{1}p_{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}}\right).

Then (
17
) follows from putting everything together. Equation
(
18
) can be shown by noting that, using Minkowski’s inequality

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\left\|\widetilde{I}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|II\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|III\right\|_{F}^{2}\right),

where
II
and
III
are defined in (
71
), and
\widetilde{I}=I_{b}+I_{c}+I_{d}
, with
I_{b}
,
I_{c}
and
\ I_{d}
defined in (
72
). The desired result can now be shown by applying the same logic
as above.
∎

Proof of Theorem
4
.

Some arguments are similar to the proof of Lemma
2
, and we
therefore omit them when possible. We begin by showing (
22
);
by definition, it holds that

\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}=\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1},

whence

(73)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}_{1}}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=I+II+III+III^{\prime}+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI.

Upon letting

\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}=\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\frac{\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}}{p_{2}}\frac{\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}}{p_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}}{p_{1}}\right)\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1},

the same logic as in the above yields that
\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}(1)
. We now carry out the proof
under
h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=1
when possible, so that
\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}
is a random
sign. It holds that

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=II_{a}+II_{b}+II_{b}^{\prime}+II_{c},

and

\left\|II_{a}\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right),

using/adapting (
55
). We now consider

\left\|II_{b}\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}.

Noting that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,h=1}^{p_{1}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\hat{c}_{j}-c_{j}\right)e_{ij,t}\right)\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}c_{j}e_{hj,t}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i,h=1}^{p_{1}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\hat{c}_{j}-c_{j}\right)e_{ij,t}\right)^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}c_{j}e_{hj,t}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i,h=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\hat{c}_{j}-c_{j}\right)^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}e_{ij,t}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}c_{j}e_{hj,s}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)\sum_{i,h=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}e_{ij,t}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}c_{j}e_{hj,s}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}e_{ij,t}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}c_{j}e_{hj,s}\right)^{2}\right),

it is easy to see that

\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\left(p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{3}\right),

whence it immediately follows that

\left\|II_{b}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{2}}\right),

and the same holds for
II_{b}^{\prime}
; similarly, it is not hard to show
that
II_{c}
is dominated by
II_{a}
and
II_{b}
. Turning to
III
,

\displaystyle III

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}=III_{a}+III_{b},

and we have

\displaystyle\left\|III_{a}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}\left(p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}\right)^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right),

and

\displaystyle\left\|III_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}\frac{p_{2}^{1/2}}{T}p_{1}^{1/2}\left(p_{1}p_{2}T^{2}\right)^{1/2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T^{2}}\right).

We now study

\displaystyle IV

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}-\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}+IV_{d}

\displaystyle=IV_{a}+IV_{b}+IV_{c}+IV_{d},

where
IV_{d}
is a remainder which, by the same logic as above, can be
shown to be dominated by
IV_{a}-IV_{c}
. It holds that

\displaystyle\left\|IV_{a}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}-\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{2}T^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F};

noting that

(74)

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}+\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{C_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1/2}}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}\right),

it now follows that

\left\|IV_{a}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}(1)\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{3/2}T^{3/2}}+\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T^{5/2}}+\frac{1}{T^{3}}\right).

Similarly, using again (
74
)

\displaystyle\left\|IV_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}T^{2}}+\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{3}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{3/2}T^{3/2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T^{5/2}}\right),

and

\displaystyle\left\|IV_{c}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{2}T^{2}}T^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{1}{\left(p_{1\wedge 2}T\right)^{1/2}}\right)\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}+\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}}{T}\right)

by the same logic as above. We now study

\displaystyle V^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}-\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{0}\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}+V_{d}

\displaystyle=V_{a}+V_{b}+V_{c}+V_{d},

where
V_{d}
is a remainder which, by the same logic as above, can be shown
to be dominated by
V_{a}-V_{c}
. We have

\displaystyle\left\|V_{a}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}-\mathbf{C}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=p_{1}^{1/2}O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)T

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)\right),

\displaystyle\left\|V_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)T

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)\right),

and, using Lemma
24

\displaystyle\left\|V_{c}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\right\|_{F}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{0}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}-\left(\mathbf{H}_{R_{0}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\left(\mathbf{H}_{C_{0}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T^{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1\wedge 2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1\wedge 2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)\right).

Finally, using (
18
)

\displaystyle\left\|VI\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)\left\|\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{1}}^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1\wedge 2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)^{2}p_{2}p_{1}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}T}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1\wedge 2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)^{2}\right),

which is dominated. The desired result now follows from putting all
together; finally, the invertibility of
\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}
can be
shown in a similar way as in the above.

As far as (
23
) is concerned, recall

\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}=\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{C_{1}}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{C_{1}}^{-1},

with

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{C_{1}}^{-1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{C_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{C_{1}}^{-1}+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{C_{1}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{C_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{C_{1}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{C_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{C_{1}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{2}p_{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{C_{1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=I+II+III+III^{\prime}+IV+IV^{\prime}+V+V^{\prime}+VI.

Upon defining

\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}=\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}}{p_{1}}\right)\left(\frac{\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}}{p_{1}}\right)\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}}{p_{2}}\right)\right)\widetilde{\Lambda}_{C_{1}}^{-1},

the proof of the theorem is the same as above, and we therefore omit it.
∎

Proof of Theorem
5
.

Recall that

\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1,t}=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathring{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}.

We will use the decompositions

(75)

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{1}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}-\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right)\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1},

(76)

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}_{1}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}\right)^{-1}-\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}\right)\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}\right)^{-1}.

It holds that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{1,t}

\displaystyle=\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}-\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right)\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}\right)\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right)\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}\right)\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}+I+II+III+IV+V.

It holds that

\displaystyle\left\|I\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{C}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{1,t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}+\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T^{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T}+\frac{1}{T^{3/2}}\right)p_{2}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}+\frac{1}{T^{3/2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}T^{1/2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T}\right);

by the same token, it can be shown that

\left\|II\right\|_{F}=O_{P}(1)\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}+\frac{1}{T^{3/2}}+\frac{1}{p_{2}T^{1/2}}+\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right),

and
\left\|III\right\|_{F}
is clearly dominated by
\left\|I\right\|_{F}
and
\left\|II\right\|_{F}
. Using the convention

h_{R_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}=h_{R_{0}}=h_{C_{0}}=1

\displaystyle IV

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}\right)+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}-\mathbf{C}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=IV_{a}+IV_{b}+IV_{c}+IV_{d}.

In the above we showed that
\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}\right)
and
\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}\right)
; hence it follows that

\left\|IV_{a}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{1/2}}\right),

and

\displaystyle\left\|IV_{b}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{1}}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{C_{1}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}p_{1}^{1/2}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}+\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{T^{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T}+\frac{1}{T^{3/2}}\right)\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}^{1/2}T}+\frac{1}{T^{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1}T}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right),

and likewise

\left\|IV_{c}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}(1)\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T}+\frac{1}{T^{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{2}T}+\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right).

Finally, it is not hard to see that
IV_{d}
is dominated by
IV_{a}-IV_{c}
. Finally, after some algebra

\displaystyle\left\|V\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{0}\mathbf{F}_{0,t}\mathbf{C}_{0}^{\prime}-\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}\hat{\mathbf{C}}_{0}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(1)\left(\frac{1}{\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)^{1/2}}+\frac{1}{p_{1\wedge 2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right).

The final result follows from putting all together. The proof of (
5
) is similar to that of (
18
), and we omit it to
save space.
∎

Proof of Lemma
3
.

Consider (
25
). The result follows immediately upon
considering the term

\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{1,\perp}^{s}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{\perp}^{s}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{0}}^{-1},

in the expansion of
\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{0}}
, which can be derived along the same lines as (
68
). In particular, the term

\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}^{s}\left(\mathbf{C}_{1,\perp}^{s}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\widetilde{\Lambda}_{R_{0}}^{-1},

is of order
O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}}{p_{2}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\right)
; again, this can be shown
following exactly the proof of (
69
). Since this is the dominant rate
in
\left\|\hat{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-\mathbf{R}_{0}\hat{\mathbf{H}}_{R_{0}}\right\|_{F}
, the desired result follows. The same arguments
yield also (
26
).
∎

Proof of Lemma
4
.

The method of proof is the same for all theorems and lemmas, and it is based
on an argument in
Bai (2003)
- see in particular Footnote 5 on p. 143. Consider
the random variable
\mathcal{A}
, with
\mathcal{A}=1
if the relevant
result holds, and
\mathcal{A}=0
otherwise; and the random variable
\mathcal{B}
, with
\mathcal{B}=1
if
\widetilde{h}_{R_{1}}=h_{R_{1}}
, and
\widetilde{h}_{C_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}
, and
\widetilde{h}_{R_{1}}=h_{R_{0}}
, and
\widetilde{h}_{C_{1}}=h_{C_{0}}
, and
\mathcal{B}=0
otherwise. Then we have

P\left(\left\{\mathcal{A}=1\right\}\right)=P\left(\left\{\mathcal{A}=1\right\}\cap\left\{\mathcal{B}=1\right\}\right)+P\left(\left\{\mathcal{A}=1\right\}\cap\left\{\mathcal{B}=0\right\}\right).

Note also that

P\left(\left\{\mathcal{A}=1\right\}\cap\left\{\mathcal{B}=0\right\}\right)\leq P\left(\left\{\mathcal{B}=0\right\}\right)=o(1),

under the assumption that
\widetilde{h}_{R_{1}}=h_{R_{1}}+o_{P}(1)
,
\widetilde{h}_{C_{1}}=h_{C_{1}}+o_{P}(1)
,
\widetilde{h}_{R_{1}}=h_{R_{0}}+o_{P}(1)
,
\widetilde{h}_{C_{1}}=h_{C_{0}}+o_{P}\left(1\right)
, which also entails that
P\left(\left\{\mathcal{B}=1\right\}\right)\rightarrow 1
. Hence we have

\displaystyle P\left(\left\{\mathcal{A}=1\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=P\left(\left\{\mathcal{A}=1\right\}\cap\left\{\mathcal{B}=1\right\}\right)+o(1)

\displaystyle=P\left(\left\{\mathcal{A}=1\right\}|\left\{\mathcal{B}=1\right\}\right)P\left(\left\{\mathcal{B}=1\right\}\right)+o(1)

\displaystyle=P\left(\left\{\mathcal{A}=1\right\}|\left\{\mathcal{B}=1\right\}\right)+o(1),

which proves the desired result.
∎

Proof of Theorem
7
.

We only show that
\widetilde{h}_{R_{1}}=h_{R_{1}}+o_{P}(1)
; the other
results follow from the same arguments. Recall that, by Lemma
25
,

(77)

\lambda_{j}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)=c_{0}+o_{P}(1),

for all
j\leq h_{R_{1}}
, where
c_{0}>0
; and, also

(78)

\lambda_{j}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1\wedge 2}^{1/2}T^{3/2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}T}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}T}\right),

for all
j>h_{R_{1}}
. Hence, by elementary arguments, (
77
) entails that

\max_{1\leq j\leq h_{R_{1}}-1}\frac{\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{M}_{X}^{R_{1}\diamond}\right)}{\lambda_{j+1}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)+\widetilde{c}_{R_{1}}\delta_{R,p_{1},p_{2},T}^{k}}\leq\max_{1\leq j\leq h_{R_{1}}-1}\frac{\lambda_{j}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)}{\lambda_{j+1}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)}=O_{P}(1).

Similarly, using (
78
) and the definition of
\widetilde{\delta}_{R,p_{1},p_{2},T}^{k}

\max_{h_{R_{1}}+1\leq j\leq h_{\max}}\frac{\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{M}_{X}^{R_{1}\diamond}\right)}{\lambda_{j+1}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)+\widetilde{c}_{R_{1}}\delta_{R,p_{1},p_{2},T}^{k}}\leq\max_{1\leq j\leq h_{R_{1}}-1}\frac{\lambda_{j}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)}{\widetilde{c}_{R_{1}}\delta_{R,p_{1},p_{2},T}^{k}}=O_{P}(1).

Finally, combining (
77
) and (
78
), as
\min\left\{p_{1},p_{2},T\right\}\rightarrow\infty
we have that, for some
0<c_{0}<\infty

P\left(\frac{\lambda_{h_{R_{1}}}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)}{\lambda_{h_{R_{1}}+1}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)+\widetilde{c}_{R_{1}}\delta_{R,p_{1},p_{2},T}^{k}}\geq c_{0}\left(\delta_{R,p_{1},p_{2},T}^{k}\right)^{-1}\lambda_{h_{R_{1}}}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)\right)=1.

The desired result follows from noting that, by (
77
)

\lim_{\min\left\{p_{1},p_{2},T\right\}\rightarrow\infty}\left(\delta_{R,p_{1},p_{2},T}^{k}\right)^{-1}\lambda_{h_{R_{1}}}\left(\mathbf{M}_{X}^{R_{1}\diamond}\right)=\infty\text{. }

When using the mock eigenvalue, note that if
h_{R_{1}}>0

\frac{\lambda_{0}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)}{\lambda_{1}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)+\widetilde{c}_{R_{1}}\delta_{R,p_{1},p_{2},T}^{k}}\leq\frac{\lambda_{0}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)}{\lambda_{1}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)}=o_{P}(1),

by the definition of
\lambda_{0}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)
;
conversely, if
h_{R_{1}}=0
, then by the same token as above

P\left(\frac{\lambda_{0}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)}{\lambda_{1}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)+\widetilde{c}_{R_{1}}\delta_{R,p_{1},p_{2},T}^{k}}\geq c_{0}\left(\delta_{R,p_{1},p_{2},T}^{k}\right)^{-1}\lambda_{0}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)\right)=1.

for some
0<c_{0}<\infty
, and, by the construction of
\lambda_{h_{R_{1}}}\left(\mathring{\mathbf{M}}_{R_{1}}\right)

\lim_{\min\left\{p_{1},p_{2},T\right\}\rightarrow\infty}\left(\delta_{R,p_{1},p_{2},T}^{k}\right)^{-1}\lambda_{0}\left(\mathbf{M}_{X}^{R_{1}\diamond}\right)=\infty\text{,}

whence the desired result again follows.
∎
